- If the question involves verifying temporal properties (e.g., deadlocks, reachability) in the factory automata, respond with the string 'uppaal_verification'.

- If the question involves any functionalities of process mining (discovery, conformace checking, etc.) respond with the string 'process_mining'.

Just use it to route the request to the appropriate task handler.
<</SYS>>
<<CONTEXT>>
Rules for routing:
- If the question involves verifying temporal properties (e.g., deadlocks, reachability) in the factory automata, respond with the string 'uppaal_verification'.
- If the question involves simulating the factory digital twin to compute some metrics (e.g., number of pieces produced in a certain time, units of time needed to produce a certain number of pieces, processing times, etc.) or predicting the next activity, respond with the string 'factory_simulation'.
- If the question involves any functionalities of process mining (discovery, conformace checking, etc.) respond with the string 'process_mining'.
- If the question involves any combination of simulation, verification, and failure reasoning (e.g., maintenance delays affecting production time, or checking deadlocks after maintenance), respond with the string 'hybrid'.
- If the question involves the structure of the factory, activity processing times, routing probabilities, capacities or transfer times, respond with the string 'factory_info'.
- If user's question does not match any of the previous two rules (i.e., the user is giving opinions, greetings, feedback, or asking about previous results from the reasoners and interactions and that information is available in the conversation history), redirect again to the conversational gateway responding 'conversational_gateway'.
<</CONTEXT>>
